Remove low frequency content\n    strain = highpass(strain, flow)\n\n    # Calculate PSD for whitening\n    psd = strain.psd(2)\n    psd = interpolate(psd, strain.delta_f)\n\n    # Inverse spectrum truncation to smooth PSD\n    psd = inverse_spectrum_truncation(psd, int(2 * strain.sample_rate), low_frequency_cutoff=flow/2.)\n\n    # Whiten the data\n    strain = strain.whiten(4, 4, low_frequency_cutoff=flow)\n\n    return strain\n\ndef highpass(strain, frequency):\n    \"\"\"Apply highpass filter to strain data\"\"\"\n    from pycbc.filter import highpass_fir\n    return highpass_fir(strain, frequency, 8)\n\ndef generate_template(approximant, mass1, mass2, sample_rate=2048, duration=128):\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate a template waveform for given parameters\n\n    Args:\n        approximant: Waveform approximant name\n        mass1: Primary mass in solar masses\n        mass2: Secondary mass in solar masses\n        sample_rate: Sampling rate (Hz)\n        duration: Duration of waveform (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        TimeSeries template waveform\n    \"\"\"\n    # Generate template in time domain\n    hp, _ = get_td_waveform(approximant=approximant,\n                           mass1=mass1,\n                           mass2=mass2,\n                           f_lower=20.0,\n                           sample_rate=sample_rate,\n                           delta_t=1.0/sample_rate)\n\n    # Pad or trim to match duration\n    required_length = int(duration * sample_rate)\n    current_length = len(hp)\n\n    if current_length > required_length:\n        # Trim to required length\n        hp = hp.truncate(required_length)\n    elif current_length < required_length:\n        # Pad with zeros\n        hp = hp.prepend_zeros(required_length - current_length)\n\n    # Resize to match required duration\n    hp = hp.resize(required_length)\n\n    return hp\n\ndef perform_matched_filtering(data, template):\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform matched filtering between data and template\n\n    Args:\n        data: TimeSeries strain data\n        template: TimeSeries template waveform\n\n    Returns:\n        Signal-to-noise ratio TimeSeries\n    \"\"\"\n    # Resize template to match data length\n    if len(template) > len(data):\n        template = template.truncate(len(data))\n    elif len(template) < len(data):\n        template = template.resize(len(data))\n\n    # Perform matched filtering\n    snr = matched_filter(template, data, low_frequency_cutoff=20.0)\n\n    return snr\n\ndef main():\n    # Load gravitational wave data\n    logging.info(\"Loading gravitational wave data...\")\n    strain = frame.read_frame('/root/data/PyCBC_T2_2.gwf', 'H1:TEST-STRAIN')\n\n    logging.info(f\"Data loaded: {len(strain)} samples at {strain.sample_rate} Hz\")\n    logging.info(f\"Duration: {strain.duration:.2f} seconds\")\n\n    # Condition the data\n    logging.info(\"Conditioning data (highpass filtering and whitening)...\")\n    strain = condition_data(strain)\n\n    # Define search parameters\n    approximants = ['SEOBNRv4_opt', 'IMRPhenomD', 'TaylorT4']\n    mass_range = range(10, 41)  # 10 to 40 solar masses (inclusive)\n\n    # Store results\n    results = []\n\n    # Process each approximant\n    for approx in approximants:\n        logging.info(f\"\\nProcessing approximant: {approx}\")\n        best_snr = 0\n        best_mass1 = 0\n        best_mass2 = 0\n        best_total_mass = 0\n\n        # Grid search over mass parameters\n        for mass1 in mass_range:\n            for mass2 in mass_range:\n                # Generate template\n                template = generate_template(approx, mass1, mass2,\n                                            sample_rate=strain.sample_rate,\n                                            duration=strain.duration)\n\n                # Perform matched filtering\n                snr = perform_matched_filtering(strain, template)\n\n                # Find peak SNR (excluding edges)\n                peak_idx = len(snr) // 4\n                end_idx = 3 * len(snr) // 4\n                snr_slice = snr[peak_idx:end_idx]\n\n                if len(snr_slice) > 0:\n                    max_snr = max(abs(snr_slice))\n\n                    # Update best match\n                    if max_snr > best_snr:\n                        best_snr = max_snr\n                        best_mass1 = mass1\n                        best_mass2 = mass2\n                        best_total_mass = mass1 + mass2\n\n                        logging.info(f\"  New best match: m1={mass1}, m2={mass2}, \"\n                                   f\"total={mass1+mass2}, SNR={max_snr:.2f}\")\n\n        # Record best result for this approximant\n        if best_snr > 0:\n            results.append({\n                'approximant': approx,\n                'snr': best_snr,\n                'total_mass': best_total_mass\n            })\n            logging.info(f\"\\nBest match for {approx}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, \"\n                        f\"Total Mass={best_total_mass} M☉\")\n        else:\n            logging.warning(f\"No signal detected for {approx}\")\n\n    # Save results to CSV\n    if results:\n        df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n        df.to_csv('/root/detection_results.csv', index=False)\n        logging.info(f\"\\nResults saved to /root/detection_results.csv\")\n\n        # Pad or trim to match duration\n    required_length = int(duration * sample_rate)\n    current_length = len(hp)\n\n    if current_length > required_length:\n        # Trim to required length\n        hp = hp.truncate(required_length)\n    elif current_length < required_length:\n        # Remove low frequency content\n    strain = highpass(strain, flow)\n\n    # Calculate PSD for whitening\n    psd = strain.psd(2)\n    psd = interpolate(psd, strain.delta_f)\n\n    # Inverse spectrum truncation to smooth PSD\n    psd = inverse_spectrum_truncation(psd, int(2 * strain.sample_rate), low_frequency_cutoff=flow/2.)\n\n    # Resize to match required duration\n        hp = hp.resize(required_length)\n\n        return hp\n    except Exception as e:\n        logging.warning(f\"Failed to generate template for {approximant}, m1={mass1}, m2={mass2}: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return None\n\ndef perform_matched_filtering(data, template):\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform matched filtering between data and template\n\n    Args:\n        data: TimeSeries strain data\n        template: TimeSeries template waveform\n\n    Returns:\n        Signal-to-noise ratio TimeSeries\n    \"\"\"\n    #
